# Skills · M · part 30

850 entries, method-gap-research-status to mf-screener. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **method-gap-research-status**: Read-only family census that recommends one skill, or none, for a target-specific method-gap research census. ([joeloverbeck/skill-evidence](https://github.com/joeloverbeck/skill-evidence/tree/HEAD/assets/skills/method-gap-research-status) · 2 copies)
- **method-pm-audit**: Playbook for auditing PM-health across 15 dimensions (Discovery, Strategy, Prioritization, Roadmap, Specs, Metrics, Launch, Measure, Comms, Decisions, Pricing, Competitive, Feedback, AI-PM, Governance) and producing a structured report. The brain behind pm-auditor. ([VandanaAjayDubey111/great-pm](https://github.com/VandanaAjayDubey111/great-pm/tree/HEAD/plugins/great-pm/skills/method-pm-audit) · ★3)
- **method-reconstruction**: Reverse-engineer a computer-science / machine-learning / mathematics paper into a runnable, traceable Python codebase when the authors released no code — the core of the toolkit. Use when the user says "the authors gave no code", "turn this paper into Python", "reconstruct this architecture/algorithm/loss from the paper", "reverse engineer this ML method so we can validate it", or when a claim needs code before it can be tested. Extracts the model ARCHITECTURE, the MATHEMATICS (losses, update rules, equations), the HYPERPARAMETERS, and the ALGORITHM/procedure, and emits a structured Python pr… ([usama-shiranai90/paper-to-code-reproducibility-agent](https://github.com/usama-shiranai90/paper-to-code-reproducibility-agent/tree/HEAD/skills/method-reconstruction) · ★1)
- **method-selector**: Build and risk-screen a compact role-based method shortlist for a mathematical-modeling subquestion. Use after problem framing and data profiling, before model code generation, to propose a main candidate, a usable baseline, and at most one conditional fallback without padding the pool. ([zhnnky329/MathModeling-skills](https://github.com/zhnnky329/MathModeling-skills/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/method-selector) · ★943 · 3 copies)
- **method-selector**: Propose a compact, evidence-backed main method, an applicable reference role, and at most one conditional fallback, then present the genuine method choice for human decision. ([cancelled0/math-modeling-harness](https://github.com/cancelled0/math-modeling-harness/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/method-selector))
- **method-transfer-engine**: Six-phase protocol for adapting methods across research domains ([brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills](https://github.com/brycewang-stanford/Auto-Empirical-Research-Skills/tree/HEAD/skills/26-Data-Wise-scholar/skills/research/method-transfer-engine) · ★3.9k · 3 copies)
- **method-writing**: Write and revise the Methods section of research papers to ensure reproducibility; use when preparing an IMRAD manuscript or responding to journal/reporting-guideline requirements (e.g., CONSORT/STROBE/PRISMA). ([aipoch/medical-research-skills](https://github.com/aipoch/medical-research-skills/tree/HEAD/scientific-skills/Academic%20Writing/method-writing) · ★1.9k)
- **method-writing**: Write or revise a paper's Method section using recipes distilled from dissecting top ML researchers' papers. Use when drafting or restructuring a method section, choosing its architecture figure, algorithm box, or notation, deciding how to introduce and defend a system's components, or fixing a method that reads as a flat wall of mechanics. Applies the N-named-parts skeleton, the setup-banks-notation rule, the star-component discipline, the running-example rule, and mechanical checklists. ([Alexyskoutnev/research-skills](https://github.com/Alexyskoutnev/research-skills/tree/HEAD/method-writing) · ★1)
- **methode**: Doctrine de travail du plugin — hiérarchie entre référentiels FWB et programmes SeGEC, régimes en vigueur (tronc commun en 1-3, programmes classiques en 4-6), règle « analyser avant de proposer », convention de traçabilité vérifié/proposé. Base de connaissances chargée par toutes les autres skills du plugin ; à charger aussi dès qu'il est question de conformité au programme, de citation d'un attendu, ou de ce qui est prescrit par rapport à ce qui est proposé. ([Hmkhalla/collegia-claude-plugins](https://github.com/Hmkhalla/collegia-claude-plugins/tree/HEAD/plugins/collegia-prof/skills/methode))
- **methode-musk**: Passer les exigences d'une idée cadrée aux premiers principes puis à l'algorithme en cinq étapes, sans perdre le besoin utilisateur ni le détail signature. À utiliser sur la carte « Méthode Musk », après un cadrage relu. ([Lugdunoob/regie-claude](https://github.com/Lugdunoob/regie-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/methode-musk))

- **mf-cloud-import**: 領収書・請求書の画像(JPG/PNG)やPDFを読み取り、ファイルのリネームとMFクラウド仕訳インポート用CSV・Excel管理ファイルを自動生成するスキル。 フォルダ内の領収書データを一括処理し、日付_店舗名の命名規則でリネーム、勘定科目の自動判定、MFクラウド形式のCSV出力まで行う。 さらに、MFクラウドに連携した銀行口座・クレジットカードの明細から仕訳を登録する手順も含む（末尾の追補セクション）。 このスキルは以下のキーワードで発動すること：領収書、請求書、レシート、仕訳、経理、MFクラウド、マネーフォワード、MoneyForward、勘定科目、取込、インポート、経費精算、帳簿、連携、カード明細、未払金、残高試算表。 ユーザーがフォルダ内に領収書や請求書があり「整理して」「取り込んで」「仕訳して」などと言った場合も発動する。 「今月の仕訳」「未仕訳を登録して」など、連携明細の処理を求められた場合も発動する。 ([lucky77lucky01250-sudo/mf-cloud-import-skill](https://github.com/lucky77lucky01250-sudo/mf-cloud-import-skill/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
